863 entries, saas-video to sage-core. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **saas-video**: Make a SaaS demo or explainer video with Pexo. Describe your software (or paste your app URL or a screenshot) and Pexo writes the script, generates the visuals, picks the models, adds narration and captions, and returns a finished, publish-ready video — for your landing page, onboarding, or sales. Use for SaaS and software videos: "saas video", "saas demo video", "software demo", "app explainer video". NOT for live screen recording — Pexo generates the visuals. ([pexoai/pexo-skills](https://github.com/pexoai/pexo-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/saas-video) · ★785)
- **saas-visual-generator**: Generate brand visuals via the Higgsfield MCP — logo variants, moodboard tiles, and a brand-guidelines document — once the brand intake (`brand.json` + `saas-context.json`) exists. Auto-activate after `saas-brand-intake` finishes, or when the user says "generate logo", "make moodboard", "create visuals", `/generate-visuals`, `/regen-logo`, `/regen-moodboard`. Never fall back to other image generators if Higgsfield is unavailable — stop and report. ([potenlab/vibes-marketing-validation](https://github.com/potenlab/vibes-marketing-validation/tree/HEAD/skills/saas-visual-generator))
- **saas-waitlist-backend**: Pick and wire the waitlist backend adapter when generating the landing page. Auto-activate when `saas-landing-builder` is scaffolding the waitlist component. Chooses between local-JSON (default), Supabase, Resend, or Loops based on env vars and user preference, then writes the matching `app/api/waitlist/route.ts` and `lib/waitlist.ts` into `sites/&lt;slug&gt;/`. ([potenlab/vibes-marketing-validation](https://github.com/potenlab/vibes-marketing-validation/tree/HEAD/skills/saas-waitlist-backend))

- **sac-capture-diagrams**: Capture wireframes and architecture diagrams into the SAC second brain as OKF concepts with Mermaid or PlantUML listings embedded in Markdown. Covers wireframes (PlantUML salt), architecture, component, sequence, activity, state machine, class, ERD, deployment, C4, and data-flow diagrams. Also use when authoring Module/Class/Method/Function concepts. ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-capture-diagrams))
- **sac-design-with**: Use the SAC architecture second brain when designing new features, services, APIs, mobile apps, web apps, or infra. Loads progressive-disclosure packs (blast radius, contracts, deps, environments) so design work reuses existing topology instead of inventing in a vacuum. Trigger on design, new service, new API, new feature, architecture impact, or "how should we build". ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-design-with))
- **sac-doctor**: One-screen health check for SAC knowledge bundles (types, thin concepts, broken links). ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-doctor))
- **sac-e-atendimento**: Estrutura tese em cima do descumprimento do SAC — Decreto 11.034/2022 (nunca citar o revogado Decreto 6.523/2008): prazos de resposta, canais e horário mínimo de atendimento telefônico, acessibilidade, gravação e histórico obrigatórios, vedação de repetir a demanda já registrada, cancelamento com efeito imediato. Cobre o descumprimento como tese do consumidor (falha de serviço, inversão de ônus da prova) e como risco administrativo do fornecedor (autuação PROCON). Aciona: SAC não resolveu ou não respondeu no prazo, empresa fez o cliente repetir a reclamação, cancelamento não foi efetivado de… ([sbroggioadv/consumidor-adv-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/consumidor-adv-os-marketplace/tree/HEAD/consumidor-adv-os/skills/sac-e-atendimento) · ★1)
- **sac-graph**: Export the architecture dependency graph as JSON or Mermaid from a SAC knowledge bundle. ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-graph))
- **sac-ingest-tickets**: Ingest Jira/Linear/Azure DevOps/GitHub Issues JSON exports into TicketLink and Feature concepts. ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-ingest-tickets))
- **sac-ingest-wiki**: Ingest Confluence/Notion/wiki Markdown exports into ADRs, glossary terms, runbooks, and discoveries. ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-ingest-wiki))
- **sac-init**: Scaffold a System Architecture Capture knowledge bundle with SAC + PKC catalogs for reverse-engineering systems into OKF. Use when starting architecture capture in a repo. ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-init))
- **sac-link**: Add typed architecture edges between SAC concepts (calls, exposes_api, deploys_to, secured_by, …). ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-link))
- **sac-pack**: Build progressive disclosure context packs (default 2-hop) for precise LLM queries over architecture knowledge. ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-pack))
- **sac-plan**: Breadth-first reverse-engineering plan — map monorepo/multi-repo roots, rank focus areas, emit deep-dive checklists. Pause here before spawning area walkers. Not query-time retrieve. ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-plan))
- **sac-retrieve**: Query-time retrieval of SAC architecture context without contaminating the parent. Spawn architecture-retriever for topology (Service, ApiContract, Package, Runtime, Pipeline, IdentityProvider, blast radius). Do not run sac_search or sac_pack in the parent. Consume only the retrieval card. ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-retrieve))
- **sac-reverse-engineer**: End-to-end reverse-engineer one or more git repositories into a SAC architecture knowledge graph (packages, services, IaC, CI/CD, IAM, identity, topology). Plan breadth-first, then fan out area walkers. Primary entry skill for SAC. ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-reverse-engineer))
- **sac-scan**: Run deterministic architecture scanners (packages, containers, IaC, K8s, CI/CD, identity) against a code root without writing knowledge. ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-scan))
- **sac-search**: Full-text search over SAC architecture knowledge concepts. ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-search))
- **sac-session**: Open or close an isolated second-brain write session (worktree + PR). Use before scanning or materializing architecture when multiple agents share one knowledge remote. ([SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/system-architecture-capture/tree/HEAD/skills/sac-session))
- **sacco-architecture-evaluation**: Structured evaluation framework for cloud architecture and infrastructure decisions. Use when choosing between technical approaches, evaluating build-vs-buy, designing cross-service integrations, selecting distribution mechanisms, or any decision involving trade-offs between security, ownership, coupling, portability, and switching costs. Triggers: "how should we...", "what's the right approach for...", "evaluate options for...", architectural proposals, infrastructure design, vendor/tool selection. ([adrien-sacco/sacco-skills](https://github.com/adrien-sacco/sacco-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sacco-architecture-evaluation) · ★1)
- **sacco-codebase-audit**: Systematic codebase audit with parallel concern-specific agents and structured findings. Use for code reviews, pre-production hardening, security audits, architecture reviews, or engineering quality assessments. Produces prioritized findings with severity, file:line references, and remediation plan. Triggers: "audit", "review the code", "hardening", "production readiness", "security review", "quality assessment", "deep pass". ([adrien-sacco/sacco-skills](https://github.com/adrien-sacco/sacco-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sacco-codebase-audit) · ★1)
- **sacco-cross-team-collaboration**: Framework for effective cross-team communication via GitHub issues and PRs. Use when a task requires work from another team, when hitting a boundary that isn't yours to cross, when requesting infrastructure changes, when proposing shared tooling, or when tracking cross-repo dependencies. Triggers: "we need infra to...", "this requires changes in another repo", "raise an issue for...", "write a brief for...", blocked by another team's work, cross-repo dependencies. ([adrien-sacco/sacco-skills](https://github.com/adrien-sacco/sacco-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sacco-cross-team-collaboration) · ★1)
- **sacco-parity-verification**: Contract test parity verification for system rewrites and technology migrations. Generates behavioral tests from a reference implementation, tracks RED/GREEN progress, produces parity reports. Use when rewriting a system in a new language/framework, migrating between platforms, or ensuring API compatibility. Triggers: "parity", "contract tests", "ensure compatibility", "rewrite", "migration", "port from X to Y". ([adrien-sacco/sacco-skills](https://github.com/adrien-sacco/sacco-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sacco-parity-verification) · ★1)
- **sacco-pattern-library**: Orchestration playbooks for chaining the sacco-* skills on bigger efforts. Browse with "show me the orchestration playbooks", or use one when a job spans multiple skills: a competitive research barrage feeding requirements, a full-stack rewrite (parity + phased execution), a production-hardening loop, turning a design doc into a phased plan, or coordinating shared concerns across teams/repos. ([adrien-sacco/sacco-skills](https://github.com/adrien-sacco/sacco-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sacco-pattern-library) · ★1)
- **sacco-phased-execution**: Manages multi-phase implementation plans with checkpoint tracking, context recovery, and phase-completion gates. Use when executing large implementation plans (3+ phases), technology migrations, system rewrites, or any build that spans multiple sessions. Triggers: "execute this plan", "phase N", "what phase are we on", "continue the build", multi-step implementation, resuming after context loss. ([adrien-sacco/sacco-skills](https://github.com/adrien-sacco/sacco-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sacco-phased-execution) · ★1)
- **sacco-research-to-plan**: Research-to-plan pipeline: dispatches multi-source research, produces structured requirements with reference IDs, derives phased architecture plan. Use when exploring a new domain, designing a new system, evaluating technology options, or when the user says "research", "figure out the best way", "look at best practices", "state of the art". Integrates with a deep-research tool for source dispatch (if available) and sacco-phased-execution for plan handoff. ([adrien-sacco/sacco-skills](https://github.com/adrien-sacco/sacco-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sacco-research-to-plan) · ★1)
- **sacco-session-checkpoint**: Automatic session state checkpointing for marathon sessions. Prevents context loss by persisting critical state to disk at milestones. Use during any session longer than 1 hour, multi-phase builds, or when context compaction is imminent. Triggers: "checkpoint", "save state", "we might lose context", session approaching context limits, resuming from a previous session, "where were we". ([adrien-sacco/sacco-skills](https://github.com/adrien-sacco/sacco-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sacco-session-checkpoint) · ★1)
- **sacco-shared-concerns-extraction**: Pattern for extracting concerns that outgrow a single repository into shared resources. Use when a strictly-scoped concern becomes needed by multiple repos or teams, when evaluating distribution mechanisms for shared artifacts (OCI, packages, git repos), or when designing cross-repo contracts. Triggers: "multiple repos need this", "how do we share X across repos", "this is duplicated in two places", vendoring vs sharing decisions, schema/config/artifact distribution. ([adrien-sacco/sacco-skills](https://github.com/adrien-sacco/sacco-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sacco-shared-concerns-extraction) · ★1)
- **sacco-socratic-architecture**: Make architecture and design decisions by reasoning through trade-offs out loud instead of jumping to an answer. Use when choosing between technical approaches, weighing build-vs-buy, designing a cross-service integration, or when you want Claude to think a decision through with you step by step. Triggers: "how should we architect...", "help me decide between...", "what's the right approach for...", "think through the trade-offs", "reason this through with me". ([adrien-sacco/sacco-skills](https://github.com/adrien-sacco/sacco-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sacco-socratic-architecture) · ★1)
